# B1 claim review items

Each item shows what B1 returns: raw text and B1 metadata. Decide relevant / not_relevant / uncertain with the v2 criterion (evals/dev/README.md), then for every claim component mark complete / partial / none in the item_claim_judgments sheet (relevant needs at least one complete or partial; not_relevant and uncertain mean none everywhere). A composite claim is complete when the top-k units together cover all its components. One item can serve both queries of a matched pair.

## b1-0224 (new): csms:67834313, page - / -

Under current regulations, shipments claiming the de minimis exemption generally require minimal data for entry purposes. With certain exceptions, shipments claiming the de minimis exemption may be entered by presenting a bill of lading, or a manifest listing each bill of lading. Under current regulations, shipments claiming the de minimis exemption generally require minimal data for entry purposes. With certain exceptions, shipments claiming the de minimis exemption may be entered by presenting a bill of lading, or a manifest listing each bill of lading.
